Keeping it meant the same\\r\\n     * name ran two different drills depending on a field, and the wrong one was\\r\\n     * what a preview showed.\\r\\n     *\\r\\n     * An assignment without `mode: \\"section\\"` is content that predates the\\r\\n     * decision. It is not deleted and nothing about it is lost; there is simply\\r\\n     * no runner for it any more, and saying so plainly beats a blank screen.\\r\\n     */\\r\\n    return (\\r\\n      <div className=\\"activity-centre min-h-screen grid place-items-center px-6\\">\\r\\n        <div className=\\"max-w-md text-center\\">\\r\\n          <p className=\\"text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-muted-foreground\\">\\r\\n            Read, Recall, Colour\\r\\n          </p>\\r\\n          <p className=\\"mt-3 text-lg text-foreground\\">\\r\\n            This one runs as a whole section now.\\r\\n          </p>\\r\\n          <p className=\\"mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground\\">\\r\\n            Pick a section from the Activity Centre to start a run.\\r\\n          </p>\\r\\n          {handleLeave && (\\r\\n            <button type=\\"button\\" className=\\"mt-6 text-sm underline\\" onClick={handleLeave}>\\r\\n              Back to the Activity Centre\\r\\n            </button>\\r\\n          )}\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n      </div>\\r\\n    );\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  // The entrance is a one-shot animation that owns the screen, so it renders\\r\\n  // before the split view rather than inside it.\\r\\n  if (world && !entered) {\\r\\n    const Entrance = world.Entrance;\\r\\n    return (\\r\\n      <div className=\\"activity-centre min-h-screen\\" data-world={world.slug}>\\r\\n        <Entrance onDone={() => setEntered(true)} title={assignment.title} />\\r\\n      </div>\\r\\n    );\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <div className=\\"activity-centre min-h-screen\\" data-world={world?.slug}>\\r\\n      <div className=\\"grid min-h-screen grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-[minmax(320px,42%)_1fr]\\">\\r\\n        {worldStage ? (\\r\\n          /* The world carries the item itself, so the rail\'s job here is only\\r\\n             the two things the world does not draw: the way out and the count.\\r\\n             Falls back to the hero panel whenever the item does not parse into\\r\\n             the world\'s domain, rather than rendering an empty world. */\\r\\n          <aside\\r\\n            ref={bandRef}\\r\\n            className=\\"ac-yard-band relative flex min-h-[200px] flex-col md:min-h-[560px] md:sticky md:top-0 md:h-screen\\"\\r\\n            data-expanded={bandExpanded ? \\"true\\" : \\"false\\"}\\r\\n          >\\r\\n            <div className=\\"flex items-center justify-between gap-3 px-5 pt-5 md:px-6\\">\\r\\n              {handleBackToIntro && <ActivityExitButton onExit={handleBackToIntro} tone=\\"light\\" />}\\r\\n              <span className=\\"text-[11px] font-semibold tabular-nums tracking-wide text-[hsl(var(--aw-soft,var(--muted-foreground)))]\\">\\r\\n                {index + 1} / {items.length}\\r\\n              </span>\\r\\n            </div>\\r\\n            <div className=\\"min-h-0 flex-1\\">{worldStage}</div>\\r\\n            {/* Phone only. The world is the reward, not the route to the answer\\r\\n                box: below md it rides as a band the student can open when they\\r\\n                want it, and the answer box stays on the first screen. */}\\r\\nimport type { ActivityAssignment } from \\"../api/types\\";\\r\\nimport { ActivityExitButton } from \\"./ActivityExitButton\\";\\r\\n\\r\\ninterface ActivityHeroPanelProps {\\r\\n  assignment: ActivityAssignment;\\r\\n  current: number; // 1-based\\r\\n  total: number;\\r\\n  /** Omitted when the runner is embedded (teacher preview), which has its own chrome. */\\r\\n  onExit?: () => void;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Sticky left rail (md+) / banner (mobile). Cover panel + thin progress bar\\r\\n * across the top, mirroring the reference quiz layout.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function ActivityHeroPanel({\\r\\n  assignment,\\r\\n  current,\\r\\n  total,\\r\\n  onExit,\\r\\n}: ActivityHeroPanelProps) {\\r\\n  const pct = total > 0 ? Math.round((current / total) * 100) : 0;\\r\\n  const tagline = assignment.instructions?.tagline;\\r\\n\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <aside className=\\"ac-hero relative isolate overflow-hidden md:sticky md:top-0 md:h-screen md:min-h-[560px]\\">\\r\\n      {/* Background image / gradient */}\\r\\n      <div className=\\"ac-hero-bg absolute inset-0 -z-10\\" aria-hidden />\\r\\n\\r\\n      {/* Top progress bar */}\\r\\n      <div className=\\"relative z-10 px-6 pt-6 md:px-10 md:pt-8\\">\\r\\n        <div className=\\"flex items-center justify-between text-white/85\\">\\r\\n          {onExit ? (\\r\\n            <ActivityExitButton onExit={onExit} tone=\\"dark\\" />\\r\\n          ) : (\\r\\n            <span className=\\"text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em]\\">\\r\\n              Activity\\r\\n            </span>\\r\\n          )}\\r\\n          <span className=\\"text-[11px] font-medium tabular-nums tracking-wide\\">\\r\\n            {current} / {total}\\r\\n          </span>\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n        <div className=\\"mt-3 h-[3px] w-full overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-white/15\\">\\r\\n          <div\\r\\n            className=\\"h-full bg-[hsl(var(--ac-emerald))] transition-[width] duration-500 ease-out\\"\\r\\n            style={{ width: `${pct}%` }}\\r\\n          />\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n      </div>\\r\\n\\r\\n      {/* Editorial content */}\\r\\n      <div className=\\"relative z-10 flex h-full flex-col px-6 pb-10 pt-8 md:px-10 md:pb-14 md:pt-24\\">\\r\\n        <h1 className=\\"ac-display max-w-[14ch] text-3xl font-bold leading-[1.05] text-white md:text-[44px]\\">\\r\\n          {assignment.title}\\r\\n        </h1>\\r\\n\\r\\n        {(tagline || assignment.subtitle) && (\\r\\n          <p className=\\"mt-4 max-w-sm text-sm leading-relaxed text-white/80 md:text-[15px]\\">\\r\\n            {tagline ?? assignment.subtitle}\\r\\n          </p>\\r\\n        )}\\r\\n      </div>\\r\\n    </aside>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\nimport { supabase } from \\"@/integrations/supabase/client\\";\\r\\nimport type { SupabaseClient } from \\"@supabase/supabase-js\\";\\r\\nimport type { ConditionalQuestion } from \\"./content\\";\\r\\nimport type { ActivityItem, Verdict } from \\"../api/types\\";\\r\\n\\r\\nexport type PracticeMode = \\"general\\" | \\"linking\\" | \\"missing-link\\" | \\"must-be-true\\";\\r\\nexport interface PracticeAnswer {\\r\\n  id: string;\\r\\n  questionId: string;\\r\\n  choiceId?: string;\\r\\n  text: string;\\r\\n  verdict: Verdict | null;\\r\\n  attempt: number;\\r\\n  revealed: boolean;\\r\\n  at: string;\\r\\n}\\r\\nexport interface PracticeRun {\\r\\n  id: string;\\r\\n  userId: string;\\r\\n  mode: PracticeMode;\\r\\n  createdAt: string;\\r\\n  updatedAt: string;\\r\\n  questions: ConditionalQuestion[];\\r\\n  general?: { sourceUrl: string; items: ActivityItem[] };\\r\\n  position: number;\\r\\n  stage: \\"intro\\" | \\"paper\\" | \\"choices\\" | \\"feedback\\" | \\"done\\";\\r\\n  /** Absolute deadline for the current General question; omitted for untimed questions. */\\r\\n  paperDeadline?: string;\\r\\n  selection: string;\\r\\n  answers: PracticeAnswer[];\\r\\n}\\r\\ninterface CachedRun { run: PracticeRun; revision: number | null; dirty: boolean; pending?: PracticeRun }\\r\\ninterface RunRow { id: string; user_id: string; snapshot: Pick<PracticeRun, \\"mode\\" | \\"questions\\" | \\"general\\" | \\"createdAt\\">; state: Omit<PracticeRun, \\"id\\" | \\"userId\\" | \\"mode\\" | \\"questions\\" | \\"general\\" | \\"createdAt\\">; revision: number }\\r\\nconst prefix = \\"lsatu:conditional-practice:v1:\\";\\r\\nconst keyFor = (userId: string, id: string) => `${prefix}${userId}:${id}`;\\r\\nconst conflict = () => new Error(\\"This session changed in another tab or device. Your device copy is kept, but it has not synced. Continue from the device with the latest session.\\");\\r\\nclass CacheError extends Error {}\\r\\nclass SyncError extends Error {}\\r\\n// JSONB returns object keys in database order. Check which condition actually starts this rule.\\",\\r\\n    },\\r\\n    {\\r\\n      id: \\"both-directions\\",\\r\\n      routes: [...direct, ...reverse(direct)],\\r\\n      verdict: \\"partial\\",\\r\\n      feedback: \\"This adds a reverse direction that the statement does not guarantee.\\",\\r\\n    },\\r\\n  ];\\r\\n\\r\\n  // Two-way rules need missing-direction choices, not a falsely rejected\\r\\n  // converse. Other multi-rule items can likewise lose one entire premise.\\r\\n  if (direct.length > 1) {\\r\\n    direct.forEach((route, index) => candidates.push({\\r\\n      id: `one-rule-${index}`,\\r\\n      routes: [route],\\r\\n      verdict: \\"partial\\",\\r\\n      feedback: \\"This keeps only part of the statement. Your diagram must express every stated rule.\\",\\r\\n    }));\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  const atoms = [...new Set(direct.flatMap((route) => [route.ifSide.text, route.thenSide.text]))];\\r\\n  const seen = new Set<string>();\\r\\n  const distinct = candidates.filter((choice) => {\\r\\n    const signature = diagramSignature(choice.routes, atoms);\\r\\n    if (seen.has(signature)) return false;\\r\\n    seen.add(signature);\\r\\n    return true;\\r\\n  }).slice(0, 5);\\r\\n  // Custom authored tautologies may not support five different diagrams.\\r\\n  // Preserve the caller\'s existing fallback rather than crash the activity.\\r\\n  if (distinct.length !== 5) return [];\\r\\n\\r\\n  return shuffled(\\r\\n    distinct.map((choice) => ({\\r\\n      ...choice,\\r\\n      responseText: diagramResponseText(choice.routes),\\r\\n    })),\\r\\n    itemOrder,\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}]

Zero when that item was never touched. Resuming has to carry this:\\r\\n   * restarting the counter at zero both hands back an attempt the student\\r\\n   * already used and writes a second `attempt_no = 1` row, which makes the log\\r\\n   * ambiguous for every later replay.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  pendingAttempts: number;\\r\\n  /** What they last wrote on that item, so a half-finished answer survives a reload. */\\r\\n  pendingText: string;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Replay an attempt log against the ordered item list. `items` must be sorted\\r\\n * by `order`; `responses` may be in any order (we group by item_order and read\\r\\n * the last attempt by attempt_no).\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function reconstructProgress(\\r\\n  items: ActivityItem[],\\r\\n  responses: ActivityItemResponse[],\\r\\n): ReconstructedProgress {\\r\\n  const byItem = new Map<number, ActivityItemResponse[]>();\\r\\n  for (const r of responses) {\\r\\n    const list = byItem.get(r.item_order);\\r\\n    if (list) list.push(r);\\r\\n    else byItem.set(r.item_order, [r]);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  for (const list of byItem.values()) {\\r\\n    list.sort((a, b) => a.attempt_no - b.attempt_no);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  const verdicts: Record<number, Verdict | null> = {};\\r\\n  const text: Record<number, string> = {};\\r\\n  let resolvedCount = 0;\\r\\n  let pendingAttempts = 0;\\r\\n  let pendingText = \\"\\";\\r\\n\\r\\n  for (const item of items) {\\r\\n    const rows = byItem.get(item.order);\\r\\n    if (!rows || rows.length === 0) break; // first untouched item: stop here\\r\\n\\r\\n    const correct = rows.find((r) => r.verdict === \\"correct\\");\\r\\n    const last = rows[rows.length - 1];\\r\\n    const hitCap = last.attempt_no >= MAX_ATTEMPTS;\\r\\n    const coachFailed = rows.some((r) => r.verdict === null);\\r\\n    const resolved = Boolean(correct) || hitCap || coachFailed;\\r\\n\\r\\n    // One sub-cap, non-correct attempt means they were mid-retry. The item is\\r\\n    // NOT resolved, so the run resumes here, but the attempt they already spent\\r\\n    // and the words they already wrote come back with it.\\r\\n    if (!resolved) {\\r\\n      pendingAttempts = last.attempt_no;\\r\\n      pendingText = last.response_text;\\r\\n      break;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    const resolver = correct ?? last;\\r\\n    verdicts[item.order] = correct ? \\"correct\\" : last.verdict;\\r\\n    text[item.order] = resolver.response_text;\\r\\n    resolvedCount += 1;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  return { verdicts, responses: text, resolvedCount, pendingAttempts, pendingText };\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *\\r\\n * Scoring\\r\\n *\\r\\n * A null verdict means the Coach could not be reached. It is not a wrong\\r\\n * answer, and it used to be scored as one: the weighted sum divided by the item\\r\\n * COUNT, so an item nobody judged was arithmetically identical to an item the\\r\\n * student got wrong. A student whose network dropped on question nine was\\r\\n * marked down for it, permanently.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * The denominator is now the number of items that actually got a verdict, and\\r\\n * the parts are carried separately so the screen can say which denominator it\\r\\n * used rather than presenting a percentage and hoping.\\r\\n * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */\\r\\n\\r\\nconst VERDICT_WEIGHT: Record<Verdict, number> = { correct: 1, partial: 0.5, incorrect: 0 };\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface RunScore {\\r\\n  /** Weighted sum over the judged items. */\\r\\n  numerator: number;\\r\\n  /** How many items reached a verdict, right or wrong. */\\r\\n  judged: number;\\r\\n  /** How many items the run contained. */\\r\\n  total: number;\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Percentage over the judged items, or null when nothing was judged at all.\\r\\n   * Null is the honest answer there: returning 0 would repeat the false\\r\\n   * judgement this function exists to remove, and it renders as \\"not scored\\".\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  percent: number | null;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** The run\'s score, with the denominator it was computed over kept in view. */\\r\\nexport function scoreFor(verdicts: Record<number, Verdict | null>, total: number): RunScore {\\r\\n  let numerator = 0;\\r\\n  let judged = 0;\\r\\n  for (const verdict of Object.values(verdicts)) {\\r\\n    if (!verdict) continue; // the Coach never answered: not a zero, not counted\\r\\n    numerator += VERDICT_WEIGHT[verdict] ?? 0;\\r\\n    judged += 1;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n"}'}]

## assistant

- Consolidate General progress in `ActivityRunner`: one percentage bar based on resolved items, including null verdicts. Suppress duplicate counts in `ActivityHeroPanel`, `ActivityQuestionStack`, and `SwitchyardStage`’s `Mileposts`.
- Saved General sessions regenerate choices from stored items, so unanswered questions automatically receive the new paired format.
- Validate persisted `selection` against regenerated choice IDs. Otherwise Check can appear enabled but silently do nothing.
- Preserve historical answer text, verdicts, and choice IDs. Display stored answer text explicitly when regenerated choices differ.

Exact locations and recommendations sent to root. No edits.

format: ItemFormat;\\r\\n  /** This Activity\'s judging brief, threaded through to the Coach. */\\r\\n  coach?: ActivityCoach;\\r\\n  /** Authored per Activity: what this drill asks the student to write. */\\r\\n  answerLabel?: string;\\r\\n  answerPlaceholder?: string;\\r\\n  /** Authored per Activity: what the credited answers after the first are called. */\\r\\n  creditedSecondaryLabel?: string;\\r\\n  /** True when the Activity\'s world already shows the prompt as written. */\\r\\n  promptInWorld?: boolean;\\r\\n  /** The world\'s local check for an answer the author already wrote down. */\\r\\n  fastPath?: (item: ActivityItem, response: string) => boolean;\\r\\n  /** A world\'s authoritative deterministic judge. */\\r\\n  localJudge?: (item: ActivityItem, response: string) => CoachVerdict;\\r\\n  /** Builds the five paper-diagram choices for the active item. */\\r\\n  paperChoices?: (item: ActivityItem) => PaperDiagramChoice[];\\r\\n  diagramSeconds?: number;\\r\\n  paperDeadline?: string;\\r\\n  phaseLabel?: string;\\r\\n  paperReady?: boolean;\\r\\n  onPaperReady?: () => void;\\r\\n  /** The world\'s structural read of the answer, recorded as telemetry only. */\\r\\n  shadowFault?: (item: ActivityItem, response: string) => string | null;\\r\\n  activeIndex: number;\\r\\n  /** Attempts already spent on the ACTIVE item in an earlier sitting. */\\r\\n  resumeAttempts?: number;\\r\\n  /** What the student had written on the active item when they left. */\\r\\n  resumeValue?: string;\\r\\n  /** Where to cache what they are typing before it is   responseText: string;\\r\\n    verdict: Verdict | null;\\r\\n    attemptNo: number;\\r\\n    judgeSource?: JudgeSource;\\r\\n    shadowFault?: string | null;\\r\\n    latencyMs?: number;\\r\\n  }) => Promise<void>;\\r\\n  onNext: () => void;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Single-question page. Advancing swaps the active item instead of showing a preview stack. */\\r\\nexport function ActivityQuestionStack({\\r\\n  items,\\r\\n  format,\\r\\n  coach,\\r\\n  answerLabel,\\r\\n  answerPlaceholder,\\r\\n  creditedSecondaryLabel,\\r\\n  promptInWorld = false,\\r\\n  fastPath,\\r\\n  localJudge,\\r\\n  paperChoices,\\r\\n  diagramSeconds,\\r\\n  paperDeadline,\\r\\n  phaseLabel,\\r\\n  paperReady,\\r\\n  onPaperReady,\\r\\n  shadowFault,\\r\\n  activeIndex,\\r\\n  resumeAttempts = 0,\\r\\n  resumeValue = \\"\\",\\r\\n  draftKeyFor,\\r\\n  onResolved,\\r\\n  onRecord,\\r\\n  onNext,\\r\\n}: ActivityQuestionStackProps) {\\r\\n  const activeItem = items[activeIndex];\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (!activeItem) return null;\\r\\n\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    /* Centring the card in a full-screen flex box is right beside the world on\\r\\n       a wide screen and wrong beneath it on a phone, where it added ~700px of\\r\\n       empty space above the question. Below md the card starts where the band\\r\\n       ends. */\\r\\n    <div className=\\"flex items-start px-5 py-6 md:min-h-screen md:items-center md:px-12 md:py-12\\">\\r\\n      <section\\r\\n        key={activeItem.order}\\r\\n        aria-current=\\"step\\"\\r\\n        className=\\"ac-question-card w-full\\"\\r\\n      >\\r\\n        <div className=\\"text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-[hsl(var(--aw-eyebrow,var(--ac-emerald-strong)))]\\">\\r\\n          Question {activeIndex + 1}\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n\\r\\n        <div className=\\"mt-4\\">\\r\\n          <ActivityItemView\\r\\n            key={activeItem.order}\\r\\n            item={activeItem}\\r\\n            format={format}\\r\\n            coach={coach}\\r\\n            answerLabel={answerLabel}\\r\\n            answerPlaceholder={answerPlaceholder}\\r\\n            creditedSecondaryLabel={creditedSecondaryLabel}\\r\\n            promptInWorld={promptInWorld}\\r\\n            fastPath={fastPath}\\r\\nimport { useEffect, useState } from \\"react\\";\\r\\nimport { motion, useReducedMotion } from \\"framer-motion\\";\\r\\nimport type { Clause, DerivedRouting, Fault, Routing } from \\"./routing\\";\\r\\nimport { differs } from \\"./routing\\";\\r\\nimport \\"./switchyard.css\\";\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * The yard\'s three states. The whole drill lives in the move between them:\\r\\n * arrival (nothing routed), the routing (clauses couple onto their tracks), and\\r\\n * the flip (the junction rotates and performs the contrapositive).\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport type YardPhase = \\"arrival\\" | \\"routed\\" | \\"flipped\\";\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * One marker per item in the run. `outcome` is the verdict the student actually\\r\\n * earned, or null for items not yet reached: the yard never renders progress it\\r\\n * cannot trace to a real answer.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport interface Milepost {\\r\\n  order: number;\\r\\n  outcome: \\"correct\\" | \\"partial\\" | \\"incorrect\\" | null;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\ninterface SwitchyardStageProps {\\r\\n  /** The original sentence, shown on the departures board. */\\r\\n  statement: string;\\r\\n  /** The credited routing, parsed from the credited answer. */\\r\\n  routing: Routing;\\r\\n  /** The authored contrapositive, shown on its plate once the junction turns. */\\r\\n  contrapositive: string;\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * The contrapositive as structure the yard can perform, or null when this\\r\\n   * item did not pass the derivability gate. Null means the junction never\\r\\n   * turns on its own and the authored plate carries the contrapositive instead:\\r\\n   * a plate is not a downgrade, it is the sentence the author wrote.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  derived?: DerivedRouting | null;\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * The indicator word (\\"only if\\", \\"unless\\"). Lit on the board only once the\\r\\n   * yard has routed, never before: no pre-commit hints.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  indicator?: string;\\r\\n  phase: YardPhase;\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * The student\'s own routing, when their answer parsed. Drives the comparison\\r\\n   * panel and the fault choreography. Omitted on a clean run.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  studentRouting?: Routing | null;\\r\\n  /** The named partial, derived by `diagnose`. Null means the yard makes no claim. */\\r\\n  fault?: Fault | null;\\r\\n  /** The run\'s mileposts. Omit and the yard renders none rather than inventing a position. */\\r\\n  mileposts?: Milepost[];\\r\\n  /** Which milepost is the current item. */\\r\\n  currentOrder?: number;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Heavy, mechanical. The flip should feel like plant, not like a UI toggle. */\\r\\nconst MECHANICAL = { duration: 0.9, ease: [0.7, 0, 0.18, 1] as const };\\r\\nconst COUPLE = { duration: 0.55, ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1] as const };\\r\\n/** The statement rolling onto the board: lighter than the plant, still weighted. */\\r\\nconst ROLL_ON = { duration: 0.7, ease: [0.16, 1, 0.3, 1] as const };\\r\\n\\r\\n  return step;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function SwitchyardStage({\\r\\n  statement,\\r\\n  routing,\\r\\n  contrapositive,\\r\\n  derived = null,\\r\\n  indicator,\\r\\n  phase,\\r\\n  studentRouting = null,\\r\\n  fault = null,\\r\\n  mileposts,\\r\\n  currentOrder,\\r\\n}: SwitchyardStageProps) {\\r\\n  const still = useReducedMotion() ?? false;\\r\\n  const routed = phase !== \\"arrival\\";\\r\\n\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * The flip, at last performed. The world was built for this beat -- the half\\r\\n   * turn, the capsule swap, the contrapositive plate -- and had never once run\\r\\n   * it, because a flipped rail would have shown the ORIGINAL clause text under\\r\\n   * an inverted lamp and contradicted the plate beneath it.\\r\\n   *\\r\\n      // Only an item that passed the derivability gate is handed a structure to\\r\\n      // perform. Everything else gets null and shows the authored plate, which\\r\\n      // is correct by construction rather than derived and hoped over.\\r\\n      const derived = flipIsDerivable(item.credited_answers) ? derivedFlip(routing) : null;\\r\\n      return (\\r\\n        <SwitchyardStage\\r\\n          statement={item.prompt}\\r\\n          routing={routing}\\r\\n          derived={derived}\\r\\n          contrapositive={item.credited_answers?.[1] ?? credited}\\r\\n          indicator={item.explanation ? indicatorOf(item.explanation) : undefined}\\r\\n          phase={answered ? \\"routed\\" : \\"arrival\\"}\\r\\n          studentRouting={studentRouting}\\r\\n          fault={answered ? diagnose(routing, studentRouting) : null}\\r\\n          mileposts={mileposts}\\r\\n          currentOrder={currentOrder}\\r\\n        />\\r\\n      );\\r\\n    },\\r\\n    fastPath: (item, response) => matchesAnyCredited(item.credited_answers, response),\\r\\n    localJudge: (item, response) => judgeConditionalResponse(item.credited_answers, response),\\r\\n    paperChoices: (item) => buildPaperDiagramChoices(item.credited_answers, item.order),\\r\\n    shadowFault: (item, response) => {\\r\\n      const credited = item.credited_answers?.[0];\\r\\n      const routing = credited ? parseRouting(credited) : null;\\r\\n      if (!routing) return null;\\r\\n      const fault = diagnose(routing, parseRouting(response));\\r\\n      return fault ? `${fault.kind}:${fault.side}` : null;\\r\\n    },\\r\\n    parserVersion: PARSER_VERSION,\\r\\n  },\\r\\n  {\\r\\n    slug: \\"find-the-conclusion\\",\\r\\n    label: \\"The Argument Atelier\\",\\r\\n    scope: \\"an argument\\",\\r\\n"}'}]
